  "command": "python3 -c \"\nimport http.server, threading, os\n\nclass Handler(http.server.SimpleHTTPRequestHandler):\n    def do_GET(self):\n        if self.path == '/main.pdf':\n            filepath = '/root/trace-verdict-study/docs/paper/main.pdf'\n            self.send_response(200)\n            self.send_header('Content-type', 'application/pdf')\n            size = os.path.getsize(filepath)\n            self.send_header('Content-Length', str(size))\n            self.end_headers()\n            with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:\n                self.wfile.write(f.read())\n        else:\n            self.send_response(404)\n            self.end_headers()\n\nserver = http.server.HTTPServer(('0.0.0.0', 18923), Handler)\nprint('Server ready on port 18923')\nt = threading.Thread(target=lambda: server.handle_request())\nt.start()\nt.join(timeout=30)\nserver.server_close()\nprint('Done')\n\" &\necho \"HTTP server started\"\n",
